## 1. What this report holds and how to read it

A collection: the member words, the Strong's codes behind them, and what the study has recorded for the occurrences of those words, from the base data through the observations and the claims into the narrative. Sections 2 and 3 say what the data is; 4 and 5 say what the study has recorded; 6 lists the phrases; 7 lists the faults found in the stored rows. Verses are in Scripture order (book in cfg_book_order, chapter, verse). The detail that does not fit a reading (every Strong's entry in full, every observation and node, the change history, the narrative text) is in the workbook, one sheet for each listing; the sheet is named where it is cited.

## 3a. What lives with the member words in their verses (pairings)

The member words are not alone in their verses. This section and the workbook sheets `Verse view`, `Pairings`, `Pairing summary` and `Verse observations` put the other words of each verse beside them, so that each word's company can be plotted and the pairings counted at the Strong's code, the surface and the collection. A type is a stored field (the entity party_kind, the negator flag, the collection of the surface, the qualifier subgroup, the morph class); a distance is the difference of the stored positions of two words (span order in the verse, not syntax); a side is before or after the member word. The meaning of a pairing, and what it does for the inner being, is read in context and is recorded in the empty `reading` column of the sheets, not computed here.

- 351 verses hold a member occurrence; they hold 6576 live verse_lexical rows, 394 of them member occurrences; 2757 pairings of a member occurrence with a typed word of its verse (a word with no type of its own, such as an article, is in `Verse view` and not in `Pairings`).
- Member occurrences with a live node: 394 of 394; typed neighbours with a live node: 1676 of 2757 pairings.

### 3a.1 The pairings by type of neighbour

| neighbour type | pairings | adjacent | near | far | before | after |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| word of another collection | 1070 | 154 | 292 | 624 | 431 | 639 |
| connective | 765 | 113 | 162 | 490 | 373 | 392 |
| qualifier candidate | 333 | 51 | 78 | 204 | 144 | 189 |
| entity (human) | 233 | 36 | 51 | 146 | 129 | 104 |
| entity (divine) | 164 | 13 | 37 | 114 | 71 | 93 |
| negator | 158 | 32 | 43 | 83 | 70 | 88 |
| member of this collection | 34 | 2 | 12 | 20 | 17 | 17 |

Bands: adjacent is a distance of up to 1 position, near up to 3, far above that (cfg_setting collection.evidence_pairs).

The member occurrences placed in a subgroup of the collection index (`collection_subgroup_surface`, #2503): 394 of 394. The subgroup level below counts the pairings by the subgroup of the member word and, for a word of another collection, of that word; while the surfaces are unplaced it shows `(unplaced)`.

### 3a.2 The same pairings at each level

| surface | 1494 | 1006 | 78 |
| subgroup | 421 | 159 | 91 |

A pair seen once cannot show a trend; the level at which pairs recur is the level at which the comparison can be read. The pairs of each level are in `Pairing summary`.

## 4. What the study has recorded for these occurrences

Of 394 occurrences, 8 have an engagement observation and 386 have none recorded. There are 775 live observations on them (4 engagement, 768 of other recorded stages, 3 of the retired catalogue stages) and 340 withdrawn.

### 4.1 Engagement: what each word does for the inner being (4 observations)

An engagement observation states, for the surface in a verse, whether it voices an inner-being operation (use, face) or is silent. Each is one statement applied to every occurrence it covers.

- **Observation 21714** (engagement, tag silence, marker not yet marked, status draft): Weigh here is weighing silver or goods on a scale; no inner operation.
  - applies to 2 occurrence(s): Ezr 8:29 (weigh), Zep 1:11 (weigh)
- **Observation 21716** (engagement, tag silence, marker not yet marked, status draft): Weighing here is the weight of a ring, a cloak or hair in shekels; no inner operation.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Gen 24:22 (weighing), Jos 7:21 (weighing), Eze 5:1 (weighing)
- **Observation 21713** (engagement, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Weigh names the inner operation of judging what is said: let the others weigh what is said.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): 1Cor 14:29 (weigh)
- **Observation 21715** (engagement, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Weighing names the inner operation of pondering: the Preacher weighing and studying and arranging proverbs.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Ecc 12:9 (weighing)

### 4.2 Other recorded observations (768)

Observations of the stages that carry a written finding (prior-capture, reading, strand-reading and the like), each with the occurrences it applies to.

- **Observation 22306** (layer-1, tag group, marker evidence, status draft): The surfaces of this group share one kind of meaning: to hold something to be so. The codes carry it as thinking and supposing (G1380, G3543, G3633, G5274, G5282), as saying within oneself (H0559, whose Qal sense includes 'to say in one's heart, to think'), as reckoning and imagining (H2803J, H1819) and as the case put forward with perhaps (H0194). The verses state what is held, often as a quoted thought: Mat 17:25, Joh 11:13, Gen 20:11, 1Sa 20:3, Job 35:2, Gen 18:28.
  - applies to 155 occurrence(s): Gen 18:28 (suppose), Gen 18:29 (suppose), Gen 18:30 (suppose), Gen 18:31 (suppose), Gen 18:32 (suppose), Gen 20:11 (thought), Gen 26:9 (thought), Gen 31:31 (thought), Gen 32:8 (thinking), Gen 32:20 (thought), Gen 38:15 (thought), Exo 2:14 (thought); and 143 more (sheet `Observation nodes`)
- **Observation 22307** (layer-1, tag group, marker not yet marked, status draft): The surfaces of this group share one kind of meaning: giving attention to a matter and going over it. The codes carry it as musing and meditating (H7878, H1897, H7881, H1900), as considering and setting the heart on a thing (H2803H, H7760K, H3820A, H7896K), as reflecting and observing (G1760, G2657), as giving heed with insight (H7919A) and as weighing up (H0239, G1252, H6424); the lexicon also lists plain weights and weighing out (H4948, H8254, H5187). The verses name what is attended to: Psa 119:15, Jos 1:8, Act 10:19, Luk 2:19, Ecc 12:9, 1Cor 14:29.

- **Observation 22380** (layer-2, tag pairing, marker not yet marked, status draft): Taking stands with the group in Gen 24:22 (took, 3 before), Jos 7:21 (took, 4 after), Eze 5:1 (take, 2 before) and Mat 1:20 (take, 15 after). - **Observation 22387** (layer-2, tag pairing, marker not yet marked, status draft): Hearing stands with the group in 2Ch 6:19 (listening, 8 after), Job 35:13 (hear, 4 before) and Act 28:22 (hear, 6 before). - **Observation 22445** (layer-2, tag relation, marker evidence, status draft): The direction of the supposing changes across the verses. In most the supposer is a named person who holds a view about another person or a state of affairs: Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Saul, David, Naaman and Hezekiah hold a quoted thought, with the human entity before the member in 72 of 118 human pairs. In Mat 17:25, Mat 18:12, Mat 21:28, Mat 22:17, Mat 22:42 and Luk 10:36 the direction is reversed: Jesus asks the hearer what he thinks. Toward God it runs in Psa 50:21 (you thought I was like yourself), Act 17:29 and Jon 1:6 (perhaps the god will give a thought to us), and from the Lord to the people in Eze 11:5.
  - applies to 89 occurrence(s): Gen 20:11 (thought), Gen 26:9 (thought), Gen 31:31 (thought), Gen 32:8 (thinking), Gen 32:20 (thought), Exo 2:14 (thought), Num 36:6 (think), Deu 15:9 (thought), Jos 22:28 (thought), Judg 3:24 (thought), Judg 15:2 (thought), 1Sa 16:6 (thought); and 77 more (sheet `Observation nodes`)
- **Observation 22446** (layer-2, tag relation, marker not yet marked, status draft): The direction of the attending changes across the verses. In Jos 1:8, Psa 63:6, Psa 119:15 and Psa 143:5 it runs from the speaker to God, the law and God's works; in Job 1:8 and 2:3 it runs from the Lord to Satan concerning Job (have you considered my servant); in Mat 1:20 and Luk 2:19 a person turns the events over inwardly; in Heb 11:11 Sarah considers God faithful. The attention goes both from a person to God and from God to a person.
  - applies to 25 occurrence(s): Gen 24:22 (weighing), Gen 24:63 (meditate), 1Ki 10:21 (considered), 1Ki 18:27 (musing), 2Ch 9:20 (considered), Ezr 8:29 (weigh), Neh 13:13 (considered), Job 1:8 (considered), Job 2:3 (considered), Job 15:4 (meditation), Psa 1:2 (meditates), Psa 19:14 (meditation); and 13 more (sheet `Observation nodes`)

- **Observation 22448** (layer-2, tag relation, marker not yet marked, status draft): The direction does not change across the verses of the group of the mind's set. In Rom 8:5 and Rom 8:6 the mind is set toward the flesh or toward the Spirit, and in Mar 8:33 toward the things of God or of man; in every verse the set is a direction toward an object, with an opposite named beside it. The only entities, in Mar 8:33, are the disciples (Jesus' disciples, whom he sees as he rebukes Peter) and God and man as what the mind is set toward.

- **Observation 22449** (layer-2, tag relation, marker not yet marked, status draft): The direction of the regard changes across the verses. - **Observation 22450** (layer-2, tag relation, marker not yet marked, status draft): The direction of the thoughts changes across the verses. - **Observation 22451** (layer-2, tag relation, marker not yet marked, status draft): The direction does not change across the verses of the devising group. In Jdg 13:3, 13:5, 13:7, Isa 7:14, Mat 1:23 and Luk 1:31 a messenger or the Lord announces to a woman that she will conceive and bear a son, and in Heb 11:11 Sarah received power to conceive; the conceiving runs from the woman to the child, and the divine entity is the source of the announcement.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): Num 5:28 (conceive), Judg 13:3 (conceive), Judg 13:5 (conceive), Judg 13:7 (conceive), Isa 7:14 (conceive), Mat 1:23 (conceive), Luk 1:31 (conceive), Heb 11:11 (conceive)
- **Observation 22452** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In Rom 8:5 the mind is directed: those who live by the flesh and those who live by the Spirit each set their minds on the things of their own way. The act is a setting of the mind toward an object, stated as done by persons of either way of living, with the same verb for both.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Rom 8:5 (minds on)
- **Observation 22453** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In Rom 8:6 the setting of the mind is stated as the mind-set itself (the noun G5427, twice): a mind-set of the flesh and a mind-set of the Spirit. The verse gives the operation once for each side of the contrast and states what each issues in.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Rom 8:6 (mind on)
- **Observation 22454** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In Mar 8:33 the setting of the mind is negated for Peter: the negator stands 1 word before the verb and reaches it, so the act toward the things of God is stated as not being done, with the things of man as what the mind is on instead.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Mar 8:33 (setting your mind)
- **Observation 22455** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In Rom 8:5 and Rom 8:6 the setting of the mind is a pursuit of its object: the mind is directed toward the flesh or toward the Spirit, and the verses set the two against each other as alternatives.
  - applies to 2 occurrence(s): Rom 8:5 (minds on), Rom 8:6 (mind on)
- **Observation 22456** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In Mar 8:33 the act is a refusal as the verse states it: the setting of the mind on the things of God is negated and its opposite object is named in the same sentence.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Mar 8:33 (setting your mind)
- **Observation 22457** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The object of the setting is named in each verse as one of a pair of opposites: the things of the flesh against the things of the Spirit (Rom 8:5), the flesh against the Spirit (Rom 8:6), the things of God against the things of man (Mar 8:33). The direction does not change in kind across the three verses; only the pair differs.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Mar 8:33 (setting your mind), Rom 8:5 (minds on), Rom 8:6 (mind on)
- **Observation 22458** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In Rom 8:5 and Rom 8:6 the setting of the mind stands beside a way of living and its issue: living by the flesh or the Spirit goes with the mind-set, and the verse states death for the one and life and peace for the other. No entity is named in these two verses; the contrast is carried by the nouns flesh and Spirit.
  - applies to 2 occurrence(s): Rom 8:5 (minds on), Rom 8:6 (mind on)
- **Observation 22459** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Mar 8:33 is a rebuke: Jesus turns, sees his disciples, and says it to Peter. The setting of the mind is therefore named in a speech of correction aimed at one person in the hearing of the others, and the entities the stored data names in the verse are the disciples, God and man.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Mar 8:33 (setting your mind)
- **Observation 22460** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Regard is given as a done act in 2Ki 3:14 (Elisha has regard for Jehoshaphat), Pro 12:10 (the righteous man's care for his beast), Psa 144:3 (the Lord regards man) and 2Cor 5:16 (Christ once regarded according to the flesh). The act is a taking account of a person or a creature, and the subjects are a prophet, a righteous man, the Lord and the apostles.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): 2Ki 3:14 (regard), Psa 144:3 (regard), Pro 12:10 (regard), 2Cor 5:16 (once regarded)
- **Observation 22461** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Regard and its counting-as are foreseen in Jer 24:5 (the Lord will regard the exiles as good), Isa 29:17 (the fruitful field shall be counted as a forest) and Rom 2:26 (the uncircumcised will be counted as circumcision). The act stands in the future and gives a standing that does not yet hold.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Isa 29:17 (regarded), Jer 24:5 (regard), Rom 2:26 (regarded)
- **Observation 22462** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Regard is asked or commanded in Psa 74:20, Psa 80:14 and 2Ch 6:19 (that the Lord have regard for the covenant, the vine and the prayer) and in 1Ti 6:1 and 1Cor 4:1 (that bondservants regard their masters as worthy, that the apostles be taken as servants). The act is put as what should be done, by God in the first three and by persons in the last two.
  - applies to 5 occurrence(s): 2Ch 6:19 (regard), Psa 74:20 (regard), Psa 80:14 (regard), 1Cor 4:1 (regard), 1Ti 6:1 (regard)
- **Observation 22463** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Regard is negated in thirteen verses: Gen 4:5, Deu 33:9, 1Sa 25:25, Job 34:27, Job 35:13, Job 37:24, Psa 28:5, Isa 5:12, Isa 13:17, Isa 33:8, Lam 4:16, 2Th 3:15 and Exo 5:9. The negator reaches the member in each (no regard, do not regard, nor does he regard), and the subjects are Cain's rival, the Levite, a lord, the wicked, God and the feasters, so the denial runs both from persons and from God.
  - applies to 13 occurrence(s): Gen 4:5 (regard), Exo 5:9 (regard), Deu 33:9 (regard), 1Sa 25:25 (regard), Job 34:27 (regard), Job 35:13 (regard), Job 37:24 (regard), Psa 28:5 (regard), Isa 5:12 (regard), Isa 13:17 (regard), Isa 33:8 (regard), Lam 4:16 (regard); and 1 more (sheet `Observation nodes`)
- **Observation 22464** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In these thirteen verses the act is a refusal as the grammar states it: the regard is withheld from an offering, a person, the Lord's works, silver, man or a prayer. The refusal is spoken of God in Gen 4:5, Job 35:13, Job 37:24 and Lam 4:16, and of persons in the others.
  - applies to 13 occurrence(s): Gen 4:5 (regard), Exo 5:9 (regard), Deu 33:9 (regard), 1Sa 25:25 (regard), Job 34:27 (regard), Job 35:13 (regard), Job 37:24 (regard), Psa 28:5 (regard), Isa 5:12 (regard), Isa 13:17 (regard), Isa 33:8 (regard), Lam 4:16 (regard); and 1 more (sheet `Observation nodes`)
- **Observation 22465** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Where regard is given or asked, the act is a pursuit of its object: the Lord is asked to look to the covenant, the vine and the prayer, Elisha acts out of regard for Jehoshaphat, and the righteous cares for his beast. The pursuit runs from persons to God's works and to persons, and from God to persons.
  - applies to 6 occurrence(s): 2Ki 3:14 (regard), 2Ch 6:19 (regard), Psa 74:20 (regard), Psa 80:14 (regard), Psa 144:3 (regard), Pro 12:10 (regard)
- **Observation 22466** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): What the regard is directed at differs by verse: the Lord's works and deeds (Psa 28:5, Isa 5:12), the covenant, the vine and the prayer (Psa 74:20, Psa 80:14, 2Ch 6:19), persons (Nabal in 1Sa 25:25, Jehoshaphat in 2Ki 3:14, the exiles in Jer 24:5, the parents in Deu 33:9), Cain's offering (Gen 4:5), an empty cry (Job 35:13) and the self-wise (Job 37:24). Persons and the works of God are the commonest objects.
  - applies to 12 occurrence(s): Gen 4:5 (regard), Deu 33:9 (regard), 1Sa 25:25 (regard), 2Ki 3:14 (regard), 2Ch 6:19 (regard), Job 35:13 (regard), Job 37:24 (regard), Psa 28:5 (regard), Psa 74:20 (regard), Psa 80:14 (regard), Isa 5:12 (regard), Jer 24:5 (regard)
- **Observation 22467** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): After 'regarded' and 'regard' the complement gives the standing: foreigners (Gen 31:15), a strange thing (Hos 8:12), the greatest (Luk 22:24), worthy of honor (1Ti 6:1). Each complement is a qualifier of the counting or regarding, naming what the person or thing is counted as.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Gen 31:15 (regarded), Hos 8:12 (regarded), Luk 22:24 (regarded), 1Ti 6:1 (regard)
- **Observation 22468** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The negator is a qualifier of the regard it reaches, turning the act into a denial: 'no' in Gen 4:5, 'not' in Job 37:24 and Psa 28:5 and Isa 5:12, 'no' in Isa 13:17 and Isa 33:8 and Lam 4:16. In each verse the negator is 1 word before or after the member and the verse states what it reaches.
  - applies to 7 occurrence(s): Gen 4:5 (regard), Job 37:24 (regard), Psa 28:5 (regard), Isa 5:12 (regard), Isa 13:17 (regard), Isa 33:8 (regard), Lam 4:16 (regard)
- **Observation 22469** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Regard is bound up with worth in three verses: it is asked not to be given to a woman or a man as worthless (1Sa 1:16, 1Sa 25:25) and God does not give it to those wise in their own conceit (Job 37:24). The worth or the conceit of the person regarded is named in the same sentence, within three words of the member.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): 1Sa 1:16 (regard), 1Sa 25:25 (regard), Job 37:24 (regard)
- **Observation 22470** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): A word of contempt stands in the same sentence as the regard in Isa 33:8 (despised, 2 before), Act 28:22 (spoken against, 9 after) and 1Ti 6:1 (reviled, 11 after). The regard is set in a verse that also names the low standing of a city, a sect or a name.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Isa 33:8 (regard), Act 28:22 (regard), 1Ti 6:1 (regard)
- **Observation 22471** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Hearing and regard are paired in 2Ch 6:19 (listening, 8 after), Job 35:13 (hear, 4 before) and Act 28:22 (hear, 6 before): the regard is asked or denied together with a hearing, of a prayer, an empty cry or the views of a sect.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): 2Ch 6:19 (regard), Job 35:13 (regard), Act 28:22 (regard)
- **Observation 22472** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The surface 'regard' also holds the prepositional idiom 'with regard to' in Act 28:22 (G4012), Col 2:16 (G3313), Ecc 3:18 (H1700) and Jdg 15:3 (H9006): these are not an act of taking account of anyone and are read in their verses as standalone. The negators H0408 and H3808 in Exo 5:9 and Job 9:21 are aligned into the unit of the surface and are read as negators, not as acts of regard.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Judg 15:3 (regard), Ecc 3:18 (regard), Act 28:22 (regard), Col 2:16 (regard)
- **Observation 22473** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Reasoning is a done act of speech in seven Acts verses: Paul reasons in the synagogue, in the marketplace, in the hall of Tyrannus and before Felix (Act 17:2, 17:17, 18:4, 18:19, 19:8, 19:9, 24:25). The verb stands in the aorist or imperfect and is spoken to hearers, with persuading beside it in several; it is an exchange of grounds in speech and not a silent act.
  - applies to 7 occurrence(s): Act 17:2 (reasoned), Act 17:17 (reasoned), Act 18:4 (reasoned), Act 18:19 (reasoned), Act 19:8 (reasoning), Act 19:9 (reasoning), Act 24:25 (reasoned)
- **Observation 22474** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In Luk 9:47 the reasoning is inward: it is in the hearts of the disciples after their dispute, and it is Jesus who knows it, the knowing standing 1 word before the member. The same noun therefore gives an unspoken reasoning here and the spoken reasoning of the Acts verses.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Luk 9:47 (reasoning)
- **Observation 22475** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In 1Cor 13:11 the reckoning is the working out of a child and is set among speaking and thinking as one of the ways of a child; the verb is a past tense and is given up when the speaker became a man.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): 1Cor 13:11 (reasoned)
- **Observation 22476** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): What the reasoning is on, where the verses give it, is the Scriptures in Act 17:2, the topics of righteousness, self-control and judgment before Felix in Act 24:25 and the kingdom of God in Act 19:8; the objects are matters of teaching put to hearers, with persuading as the aim in Act 18:4, 19:8.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Act 17:2 (reasoned), Act 18:4 (reasoned), Act 19:8 (reasoning), Act 24:25 (reasoned)
- **Observation 22477** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In the ground phrases 'for this reason' and 'the reason why' (G1223, G5124, G5484, G1352, G0156) the member names the ground on which an act or state stands: Paul's sending to learn of faith (1Th 3:5), the comfort he received (1Th 3:7), the mercy received (1Ti 1:16), his imprisonment and prayer (Eph 3:1, Eph 3:14), his hindering (Rom 15:22), the chain he wears (Act 28:20) and the reminder to Timothy (2Ti 1:6). In each the ground is stated first and what rests on it follows, 8 to 13 words later where the stored positions are given.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): Act 28:20 (reason), Rom 15:22 (reason), Eph 3:1 (reason), Eph 3:14 (reason), 1Th 3:5 (reason), 1Th 3:7 (reason), 1Ti 1:16 (reason), 2Ti 1:6 (reason)
- **Observation 22478** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The ground is set beside bearing and sin in four verses: sin is not borne by reason of the offering (Num 18:32), Paul could bear it no longer and sent (1Th 3:5), the sins are what the reasoning together addresses (Isa 1:18), and the Son of God appeared on the ground of the devil's sinning (1Jo 3:8). The reasoning or the ground is thus named where a burden or a sin stands within a few words.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Num 18:32 (reason), Isa 1:18 (reason), 1Th 3:5 (reason), 1Jo 3:8 (reason)
- **Observation 22479** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Knowing is stated as the ground of error in Mar 12:24: the ground of the error is a lack of knowledge, of the Scriptures and of God's power, and the negator reaches the knowing. In Luk 9:47 the knowing is Jesus' knowing of the disciples' reasoning. The ground and the knowing stand within two words of each other in both verses.
  - applies to 2 occurrence(s): Mar 12:24 (reason), Luk 9:47 (reasoning)
- **Observation 22480** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The ground is denied in four verses: 'for no reason' (Job 1:9, Pro 3:30), no reason for the death penalty (Act 28:18) and the question whether this is not the reason (Mar 12:24). The negator reaches the reason itself in the first three, so the ground is stated as absent; in Mar 12:24 the question puts it as the answer.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Job 1:9 (reason), Pro 3:30 (reason), Mar 12:24 (reason), Act 28:18 (reason)
- **Observation 22481** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Where the ground is denied (Job 1:9, Pro 3:30, Act 28:18) the act is a refusal of a ground: Satan asks whether Job fears God for nothing, the proverb forbids contending without cause, and the Romans found no ground for the penalty.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Job 1:9 (reason), Pro 3:30 (reason), Act 28:18 (reason)
- **Observation 22482** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Two qualifiers give the reasoning its manner and length: 'three' Sabbath days in Act 17:2 (2 words before) gives how long it went on, and 'like a child' in 1Cor 13:11 (1 before and 2 after) gives the kind of reckoning. The qualifier of the ground in Rom 15:22 is 'often', which gives the repeated hindering the reason accounts for.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Act 17:2 (reasoned), Rom 15:22 (reason), 1Cor 13:11 (reasoned)
- **Observation 22483** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Reasoning and the ground run in different directions across the verses: from Paul to hearers in the Acts verses, from Jesus to those he answers in Mar 12:24, from the Lord to the people in Isa 1:18, and from God to Paul in 1Ti 1:16, where mercy is received for this reason. God and Jesus are named more often than any human in these verses, and Paul, who reasons in the Acts verses, is not among the stored entities.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Isa 1:18 (reason), Mar 12:24 (reason), Act 24:25 (reasoned), 1Ti 1:16 (reason)
- **Observation 22484** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Three words in the units of 'reason' do not carry the sense of the group: the copula H1961 in Eze 19:10 (flagged in #2533), the function words G2596 and G0302 in Act 18:14 and the adjective G2138 in Jam 3:17 ('open to reason', compliant). They are read as standalone in their verses; the causal phrase 'for this reason', which makes up most of the surface 'reason', is a ground and not a thought, which the double-meaning observation records.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Eze 19:10 (reason), Act 18:14 (reason), Jam 3:17 (reason)
- **Observation 22485** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Attending is a done act of musing on a named matter in Psa 1:2, Psa 63:6, Psa 143:5, Gen 24:63 and Pro 15:28 (how to answer), and of pondering in Luk 2:19, Act 10:19 and Mat 1:20 (the vision, these things). The verbs of musing and pondering are the same kind of act across the codes: a going over of something present to the mind, kept in the heart in Luk 2:19 and Pro 15:28.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): Gen 24:63 (meditate), Psa 1:2 (meditates), Psa 63:6 (meditate), Psa 143:5 (meditate), Pro 15:28 (ponders), Mat 1:20 (considered), Luk 2:19 (pondering), Act 10:19 (pondering)
- **Observation 22486** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The psalmist's meditating is foreseen as a resolve in Psa 77:12, Psa 119:15, 119:23, 119:27, 119:48, 119:78, 119:148 and Psa 145:5 (I will meditate). In seven of these the object is the Lord's precepts, statutes, promise, works or deeds, within two words of the member; the act is a purposed going over of what God has said or done.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): Psa 77:12 (meditate), Psa 119:15 (meditate), Psa 119:23 (meditate), Psa 119:27 (meditate), Psa 119:48 (meditate), Psa 119:78 (meditate), Psa 119:148 (meditate), Psa 145:5 (meditate)
- **Observation 22487** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Attending is commanded in Jos 1:8 (the Book of the Law is to be meditated on day and night) and in 1Cor 14:29 (the others are to weigh what the prophets say). The negator of Jos 1:8 reaches 'depart' (the Book shall not depart from the mouth), so the command to meditate stands beside a command that the Law not depart.
  - applies to 2 occurrence(s): Jos 1:8 (meditate), 1Cor 14:29 (weigh)
- **Observation 22488** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The Lord's own attending is stated in Job 1:8 and Job 2:3 (the Lord asks Satan whether he has set his heart on his servant Job) and in Pro 5:21 (the Lord weighs all a man's paths). The act is a looking at a person or a person's ways by the Lord, with Job as the object in the first two and the man's paths in the third.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Job 1:8 (considered), Job 2:3 (considered), Pro 5:21 (ponders)
- **Observation 22489** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Considering and counting as a done act appears in Neh 13:13 and 2Ch 9:20 (reckoned reliable, silver reckoned as nothing), Pro 17:28 (the silent fool counted wise), Heb 11:11, 11:19 and 11:26 (Sarah counting him faithful, Abraham concluding that God can raise the dead, Moses counting the reproach of Christ greater than treasures) and Mar 10:42 (those reputed to rule). The act is a judgment of standing or worth.
  - applies to 7 occurrence(s): 2Ch 9:20 (considered), Neh 13:13 (considered), Pro 17:28 (considered), Mar 10:42 (considered), Heb 11:11 (considered), Heb 11:19 (considered), Heb 11:26 (considered)
- **Observation 22490** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In 1Ki 10:21 the reckoning is negated: silver was not counted as anything in Solomon's days, and the negator reaches 'considered'. It is the only negated member of the group in the stored pairs that the negator reaches directly; in Jos 1:8, Eze 18:28, Mat 1:20 and Rom 4:19 the negators reach other verbs.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): 1Ki 10:21 (considered)
- **Observation 22491** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In 1Ki 10:21 the act is a refusal of worth: silver is counted as nothing, which the verse sets against the gold of Solomon's vessels.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): 1Ki 10:21 (considered)
- **Observation 22492** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Where the attending is of the law, God or his works it is a pursuit of its object: the love of the commandments comes within three words before the meditating in Psa 119:48 and Psa 119:97, and the psalmist's remembering stands 3 words before the meditating in Psa 63:6, Psa 77:3, Psa 77:6 and Psa 143:5. The attention is drawn toward what is loved or remembered.
  - applies to 6 occurrence(s): Psa 63:6 (meditate), Psa 77:3 (meditate), Psa 77:6 (meditate), Psa 119:48 (meditate), Psa 119:97 (meditation), Psa 143:5 (meditate)
- **Observation 22493** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The object of the meditating is the Lord's law and precepts in Jos 1:8, Psa 119:15, 119:27 and 119:78 (precepts 1 to 2 words from the member), his works and deeds in Psa 77:12, Psa 143:5 and Psa 145:5 (works 1 to 4 words), and the Lord himself and the days of old in Psa 63:6 and Psa 143:5. The object is God, what he has said and what he has done.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): Jos 1:8 (meditate), Psa 63:6 (meditate), Psa 77:12 (meditate), Psa 119:15 (meditate), Psa 119:27 (meditate), Psa 119:78 (meditate), Psa 143:5 (meditate), Psa 145:5 (meditate)
- **Observation 22494** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In Psa 38:12 the same verb of meditating has harm as its object: those who seek the speaker's hurt speak of ruin (1 word before) and meditate treachery, and in Psa 119:78 the speaker meditates on the precepts after he has been wronged (3 words before). The verb takes harm as its object in the first and is set against wrongdoing in the second.
  - applies to 2 occurrence(s): Psa 38:12 (meditate), Psa 119:78 (meditate)
- **Observation 22495** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The object of the considering is a person or the person's body in Job 1:8 and 2:3 (the servant Job), Rom 4:19 (Abraham's own body, as good as dead), Heb 11:11 (the one who promised), Eze 18:28 (the transgressions turned from), Ecc 2:11 (the work of his own hands) and Dan 7:8 (the horns). The objects are persons, bodies, deeds and the things seen in a vision.
  - applies to 6 occurrence(s): Job 1:8 (considered), Ecc 2:11 (considered), Eze 18:28 (considered), Dan 7:8 (considered), Rom 4:19 (considered), Heb 11:11 (considered)
- **Observation 22496** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The heart is named within three words of the member in five verses: 'the meditation of my heart' (Psa 19:14, Psa 49:3, each 1 word), 'in my heart' (Psa 77:6, 2 words), the heart of the righteous that ponders (Pro 15:28, 2 words before) and Mary pondering in her heart (Luk 2:19, 3 words). The verses place the attending in the heart.
  - applies to 5 occurrence(s): Psa 19:14 (meditation), Psa 49:3 (meditation), Psa 77:6 (meditate), Pro 15:28 (ponders), Luk 2:19 (pondering)
- **Observation 22497** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Remembering stands exactly 3 words before the meditating in Psa 63:6, Psa 77:3, Psa 77:6 and Psa 143:5: the remembering of God, of a song or of the days of old comes first in the verse and the meditating follows it in the same sentence.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Psa 63:6 (meditate), Psa 77:3 (meditate), Psa 77:6 (meditate), Psa 143:5 (meditate)
- **Observation 22498** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): A second verb of musing stands in the same clause in Psa 77:12 (ponder, 3 before) and Psa 143:5 (ponder, 3 after), and the meditation of the heart is set between wisdom (1 before) and understanding (2 after) in Psa 49:3; in Pro 17:28 the silent fool is counted wise and intelligent. Wisdom and understanding stand beside the attending within four words in these verses.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Psa 49:3 (meditation), Psa 77:12 (meditate), Psa 143:5 (meditate), Pro 17:28 (considered)
- **Observation 22499** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Searching stands near the meditating in Psa 77:6 (search, 4 after, after the meditating), Psa 38:12 (seeking 7 and 4 before) and Act 10:19 (the men looking for Peter, 8 after the pondering). The search follows the attending in the first and the third and comes before it in the second.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Psa 38:12 (meditate), Psa 77:6 (meditate), Act 10:19 (pondering)
- **Observation 22500** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Life and death are stated beside the considering in Eze 18:28 (the one who considers and turns away shall live, 8 after), Rom 4:19 (his body as good as dead, 4 after) and Heb 11:19 (raised from the dead, 7 after). In the last two what is considered is the state of death; in the first life is what follows the considering.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Eze 18:28 (considered), Rom 4:19 (considered), Heb 11:19 (considered)
- **Observation 22501** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Fear and evil stand with the considering in Job 1:8 and Job 2:3 (the fear of God 9 to 10 words after, evil turned from 12 to 13 after), in Job 15:4 (fear of God and meditation both hindered, 2 words) and in Pro 15:28 (the wicked 3 words after the ponder). The attention of the Lord rests on one who fears God and turns from evil; the meditation is set beside what is done away with.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Job 1:8 (considered), Job 2:3 (considered), Job 15:4 (meditation), Pro 15:28 (ponders)
- **Observation 22502** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The nouns for meditation name the musing itself as a thing offered, loved or hindered: it is the meditation of the heart that is asked to be acceptable (Psa 19:14), that shall be understanding (Psa 49:3), that is asked to be pleasing (Psa 104:34), the law that is the speaker's meditation (Psa 119:97, Psa 119:99), and what is hindered before God (Job 15:4). Elijah's mockery names the god's musing (1Ki 18:27).
  - applies to 7 occurrence(s): 1Ki 18:27 (musing), Job 15:4 (meditation), Psa 19:14 (meditation), Psa 49:3 (meditation), Psa 104:34 (meditation), Psa 119:97 (meditation), Psa 119:99 (meditation)
- **Observation 22503** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Weighing up as an evaluative act appears in Ecc 12:9, where the Preacher weighs proverbs with care and wisdom stands 6 words before; the physical weighing out of Ezr 8:29 is a counting of vessels before the priests. The evaluative weighing is one act of the attention of the Preacher among teaching, studying and arranging.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Ecc 12:9 (weighing)
- **Observation 22504** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The surface 'weighing' is a weight in Gen 24:22 (twice), Jos 7:21 and Eze 5:1, a measure of shekels and not an act of the mind, and the surface 'weigh' is a physical weighing out in Ezr 8:29 and Zep 1:11 (laden); 'taking' stands beside the weights in the first three (took, 2 to 4 words). These occurrences are read as standalone: they are in the group because the surface also carries an evaluative use (1Cor 14:29, Ecc 12:9), and their placement was left as uncertain in the grouping.
  - applies to 5 occurrence(s): Gen 24:22 (weighing), Jos 7:21 (weighing), Ezr 8:29 (weigh), Eze 5:1 (weighing), Zep 1:11 (weigh)
- **Observation 22505** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The stored sense head of H6424 in Pro 5:21 reads 'to envy' against a lexicon range of weigh and make level (flagged in #2533), and the codes H0595 and H1934 in Pro 24:32 and Dan 7:8 are the pronoun I and a verb of being aligned into the units of 'considered'. They are not acts of attention; the verse acts are the verbs of setting the heart and contemplating beside them.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Pro 5:21 (ponders), Pro 24:32 (considered), Dan 7:8 (considered)
- **Observation 22506** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): A thought is said within oneself and given as quoted speech in twenty-eight verses: Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Saul, David, Naaman, Hezekiah and others each think something and the verse gives it. The code behind most is the verb 'to say', whose lexicon sense includes saying in one's heart; in several the thought is spoken to another in a dialogue.
  - applies to 28 occurrence(s): Gen 20:11 (thought), Gen 26:9 (thought), Gen 32:8 (thinking), Gen 32:20 (thought), Exo 2:14 (thought), Jos 22:28 (thought), Judg 3:24 (thought), 1Sa 16:6 (thought), 1Sa 18:11 (thought), 1Sa 18:17 (thought), 1Sa 18:21 (thought), 1Sa 20:3 (thinks); and 16 more (sheet `Observation nodes`)
- **Observation 22507** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): A supposition is held about another person or a state of affairs and acted on, and in the verses of the Gospels and Acts it proves mistaken or unverified: Mary supposed to be going to the tomb, the gardener, the group on the journey, Paul dead, the prisoners escaped, a ghost, a spirit, a sleep, a purchase, a vision. In each the verb of thinking states the supposition and the verse then states what the people did or found.
  - applies to 13 occurrence(s): Mat 20:10 (thought), Mar 6:49 (thought), Luk 2:44 (supposing), Luk 24:37 (thought), Joh 11:13 (thought), Joh 11:31 (supposing), Joh 13:29 (thought), Joh 20:15 (supposing), Act 8:20 (thought), Act 12:9 (thought), Act 14:19 (supposing), Act 16:27 (supposing); and 1 more (sheet `Observation nodes`)
- **Observation 22508** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): A person holds a view of himself, which the verse then tests: that he is wise (1Cor 3:18), something (Gal 6:3), religious (Jam 1:26), a prophet (1Cor 14:37), standing (1Cor 10:12), with grounds for confidence (Phili 3:4), and that he knows (1Cor 8:2). The verb states a self-estimate, and the verse sets it beside deceiving oneself, folly or knowledge.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): Luk 8:18 (thinks), 1Cor 3:18 (thinks), 1Cor 8:2 (imagines), 1Cor 10:12 (thinks), 1Cor 14:37 (thinks), Gal 6:3 (thinks), Phili 3:4 (thinks), Jam 1:26 (thinks)
- **Observation 22509** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): A thought is held as a purpose or plan in Num 33:56, 1Sa 18:25, 2Sa 21:16, 2Ch 13:8, 2Ch 32:1, Isa 10:7, Jer 23:27, Dan 7:25, Rut 4:4 and Phili 1:17: the Lord's purpose, Saul's snare, a giant's killing, withstanding a kingdom, conquering cities, destroying nations, making the people forget, changing the times, telling a kinsman and afflicting Paul. The verb is the same as for a supposition, and the verse gives the purpose within two words of it in several.
  - applies to 10 occurrence(s): Num 33:56 (thought), Rut 4:4 (thought), 1Sa 18:25 (thought), 2Sa 21:16 (thought), 2Ch 13:8 (think), 2Ch 32:1 (thinking), Isa 10:7 (think), Jer 23:27 (think), Dan 7:25 (think), Phili 1:17 (thinking)
- **Observation 22510** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): A view is asked of the hearer in eight verses, where Jesus or the writer puts the question and the hearer's opinion is what is sought: what do you think, Simon (Mat 17:25), a man with a hundred sheep (Mat 18:12), a man with two sons (Mat 21:28), is it lawful (Mat 22:17), what of the Christ (Mat 22:42), which of the three (Luk 10:36), the feast (Joh 11:56) and how much worse a punishment (Heb 10:29). The direction of the asking runs from the speaker to the hearer.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): Mat 17:25 (think), Mat 18:12 (think), Mat 21:28 (think), Mat 22:17 (think), Mat 22:42 (think), Luk 10:36 (think), Joh 11:56 (think), Heb 10:29 (think)
- **Observation 22511** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The supposition is forbidden, denied or withheld in twelve verses: do not think (Mat 5:17, Mat 10:34, Joh 5:45), we ought not to think (Act 17:29), that person must not suppose (Jam 1:7), let no one think me foolish (2Cor 11:16), let not my lord suppose (2Sa 13:32, 2Sa 13:33), not to think too highly (Rom 12:3, twice), do not think to yourself (Est 4:13), the heart does not so think (Isa 10:7) and no thought of her future (Lam 1:9). In each the negator reaches the member itself, one to seven words from it.
  - applies to 12 occurrence(s): 2Sa 13:32 (suppose), 2Sa 13:33 (suppose), Est 4:13 (think), Isa 10:7 (think), Lam 1:9 (thought), Mat 5:17 (think), Mat 10:34 (think), Joh 5:45 (think), Act 17:29 (think), Rom 12:3 (think), 2Cor 11:16 (think), Jam 1:7 (suppose)
- **Observation 22512** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In these verses the act is a refusal as the grammar states it: Jesus rules out the supposition that he came to abolish the Law or bring peace, accuse you, or that God is like an image; Paul rules out thinking too highly of oneself and asks that no one think him foolish. The refusal is of a supposition about Jesus, about God or about oneself, and is spoken by Jesus, by Paul and by the one counselling.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): Est 4:13 (think), Mat 5:17 (think), Mat 10:34 (think), Joh 5:45 (think), Act 17:29 (think), Rom 12:3 (think), 2Cor 11:16 (think), Jam 1:7 (suppose)
- **Observation 22513** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Where the supposition is held and acted on the act is a pursuit of its content: the people follow Mary out, go a day's journey, stone Paul, draw the sword, sail on, send a gift, give a daughter to a snare. The thought is the ground of a move toward the thing supposed.
  - applies to 7 occurrence(s): Gen 32:20 (thought), 1Sa 18:21 (thought), Luk 2:44 (supposing), Joh 11:31 (supposing), Act 14:19 (supposing), Act 16:27 (supposing), Act 27:13 (supposing)
- **Observation 22514** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): What is held to be so is dying or perishing in Gen 26:9, Job 29:18 and Jon 1:6 (the dying stands 2 words after the member and is the content of the thought), escaping in Gen 32:8, Est 4:13, Act 16:27 and Rom 2:3 (the escape is within ten words and is part of what is supposed), and someone's being dead in 2Sa 13:32, 2Sa 13:33 and Act 14:19.
  - applies to 10 occurrence(s): Gen 26:9 (thought), Gen 32:8 (thinking), 2Sa 13:32 (suppose), 2Sa 13:33 (suppose), Est 4:13 (think), Job 29:18 (thought), Jon 1:6 (thought), Act 14:19 (supposing), Act 16:27 (supposing), Rom 2:3 (suppose)
- **Observation 22515** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): What is held to be so is peace and security in 2Ki 20:19 and Isa 39:8 (Hezekiah's thought, security 3 to 4 words after) and in Mat 10:34 and Luk 12:51 (the supposition that Jesus came to bring peace, which is corrected); and fortified cities in 2Ch 32:1 and a guard in Jer 40:5. The same word peace is held by Hezekiah and denied of Jesus' coming.
  - applies to 6 occurrence(s): 2Ki 20:19 (thought), 2Ch 32:1 (thinking), Isa 39:8 (thought), Jer 40:5 (think), Mat 10:34 (think), Luk 12:51 (think)
- **Observation 22516** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): What is held to be so is a state of the one who holds it: wise (1Cor 3:18, the wise within 2 words), religious (Jam 1:26), something when he is nothing (Gal 6:3), a prophet (1Cor 14:37), standing (1Cor 10:12), knowing (1Cor 8:2), with reason for confidence (Phili 3:4, confidence 6 before and 1 after) and thinking highly of oneself (Rom 12:3, faith 6 after). In each the object of the thinking is the thinker.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): Rom 12:3 (think), 1Cor 3:18 (thinks), 1Cor 8:2 (imagines), 1Cor 10:12 (thinks), 1Cor 14:37 (thinks), Gal 6:3 (thinks), Phili 3:4 (thinks), Jam 1:26 (thinks)
- **Observation 22517** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): What is held to be so is another person's state or act: a prostitute (Gen 38:15, her face covered 2 words after), a gardener (Joh 20:15), in the group (Luk 2:44), drunk (Act 2:15), dead (Act 14:19), a sleep (Joh 11:13), a ghost (Mar 6:49), unclean (Rom 14:14, the uncleanness 2 words before) and not clean (1Sa 20:26). The object is a person or a thing in a state the speaker has not verified.
  - applies to 9 occurrence(s): Gen 38:15 (thought), 1Sa 20:26 (thought), Mar 6:49 (thought), Luk 2:44 (supposing), Joh 11:13 (thought), Joh 20:15 (supposing), Act 2:15 (suppose), Act 14:19 (supposing), Rom 14:14 (thinks)
- **Observation 22518** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): What is thought of is the steadfast love of the Lord in Psa 48:9 (1 word after the member), and what answers the thought of falling is the same steadfast love in Psa 94:18 (3 words after); the poor are the ones the Lord thinks of in Psa 40:17 (poor 3 before, needy 2 before) and the ones the unworthy thought turns from in Deu 15:9 (poor 10 after). The thinking is directed at the Lord's love or at the poor.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Deu 15:9 (thought), Psa 40:17 (thought), Psa 48:9 (thought), Psa 94:18 (thought)
- **Observation 22519** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The outward act that follows a supposition is stated in the same verse: the Jews follow Mary (Joh 11:31, going 2 words after), Joseph's people go a day's journey (Luk 2:44), the crowds stone Paul (Act 14:19), the jailer draws his sword (Act 16:27, killing 2 words before), the sailors weigh anchor (Act 27:13), Saul gives his daughter as a snare (1Sa 18:21, give 1 after), and Jacob sends a present (Gen 32:20, present 3 after). The supposition and the act it leads to stand in the one sentence.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): Gen 32:20 (thought), Gen 38:15 (thought), 1Sa 18:21 (thought), Luk 2:44 (supposing), Joh 11:31 (supposing), Act 14:19 (supposing), Act 16:27 (supposing), Act 27:13 (supposing)
- **Observation 22520** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): A supposition issues in a spoken answer or a question in the exchanges of Gen 18:29 to 18:32, Gen 31:31, Exo 2:14, Est 4:13, Luk 7:43 and Luk 13:2: the supposition is put in the speaker's own words as part of a dialogue, with 'answered' 1 to 13 words from the member.
  - applies to 9 occurrence(s): Gen 18:29 (suppose), Gen 18:30 (suppose), Gen 18:31 (suppose), Gen 18:32 (suppose), Gen 31:31 (thought), Exo 2:14 (thought), Est 4:13 (think), Luk 7:43 (suppose), Luk 13:2 (think)
- **Observation 22521** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Fear stands within three words before the thought in Gen 31:31, Exo 2:14, 2Sa 14:15 and Neh 6:9, in Luk 24:37 (startled and frightened, 3 and 1 words before) and in Gen 20:11 (the fear of God, 3 words after). In five of the six the fear is named before the thought and the thought comes from it.
  - applies to 6 occurrence(s): Gen 20:11 (thought), Gen 31:31 (thought), Exo 2:14 (thought), 2Sa 14:15 (thought), Neh 6:9 (thinking), Luk 24:37 (thought)
- **Observation 22522** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Anger, hiding and hunger stand beside the thought in Gen 18:30 and Gen 18:32 (the Lord's anger), 2Ki 5:11 (Naaman's anger, 4 words before), Psa 64:5 (secretly, 1 word before), 2Ki 7:12 (hide, 2 words before; hungry, 6 before) and Gen 38:15 (the covering of her face, 2 words after). The thought arises in a person who is angry, hiding or hungry, or who is faced with something concealed.

- **Observation 22523** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Affliction, suffering and crying stand with the thought in Lam 1:9 (affliction), 1Cor 7:26 (the present distress, 3 words after), Phili 1:17 (afflict, 1 after), 1Pe 4:1 (Christ suffered, within 2 words), Luk 13:2 (suffered), Mar 6:49 (cried out, 4 after), Joh 11:31 and Joh 20:15 (weeping) and Deu 15:9 (the cry to the Lord, 14 after). - **Observation 22526** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Trust, loyalty and silence stand with the thought in 1Sa 27:12 (Achish trusted David, 2 words before), 2Ki 18:20 and Isa 36:5 (whom do you trust, 8 after), Mic 2:8 (trustingly, 1 before), Psa 48:9 and Psa 94:18 (steadfast love within 3 words) and Psa 50:21 (silence 1 word before the false supposition). Trust is the ground of Achish's thought, and the Lord's silence is the ground of the supposition that he is like the one who has done these things.
  - applies to 7 occurrence(s): 1Sa 27:12 (thinking), 2Ki 18:20 (think), Psa 48:9 (thought), Psa 50:21 (thought), Psa 94:18 (thought), Isa 36:5 (think), Mic 2:8 (thought)
- **Observation 22527** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Asking and calling stand with the supposition in Mat 6:7 (those who pray suppose they will be heard), Mat 26:53 (the supposition whether Jesus cannot appeal), 2Sa 14:15 (the request the woman came to make), 2Ki 5:11 (Naaman supposes the prophet will call on the name of the Lord, 4 after), Jer 3:19 (call me Father, 1 after) and Jon 1:6 (call out to your god, 4 before the hope). The supposition is about being heard or about a call.

- **Observation 22528** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Searching and finding stand with the supposition in Gen 18:28 and Gen 18:30 ('find' is the condition of each supposed number), Mat 18:12, Luk 2:44 and Joh 20:15 (the search follows the supposition or the question) and Joh 5:39 (searching the Scriptures, 4 words before the supposition). The supposition is set in a verse of a search or of a condition to be found.

- **Observation 22529** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Sin, judging and evil stand with the supposition in Luk 13:2 (worse sinners), Eze 18:14 (a son who sees his father's sins), Rom 2:3 (you who judge), Joh 5:45 (accuse), Mat 9:4 (evil, 1 word after), Psa 64:5 (evil, 5 before), 1Cor 14:20 (evil, 2 before) and Pro 21:29 (the wicked, 5 before). - **Observation 22530** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Believing, honor, reward and the commandment stand with the thought in Pro 14:15 (the simple believes, 3 words before), Rom 12:3 (faith, 6 after), Jam 1:26 (religion, 12 after), 2Sa 10:3 and 1Ch 19:3 (honoring, 4 after), 1Cor 12:23 (honor, 3 after), Heb 10:29 (punishment, 1 before), Mat 22:17 (pay, 2 after) and Num 36:6 and 1Cor 14:37 (the Lord's command). In each the thought is about something owed, believed or commanded.

- **Observation 22531** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Truth, righteousness and the lawful stand with the supposition in Job 35:2 (just, 2 after), Mat 22:17 (lawful, 1 after), 2Cor 12:6 (truth, 5 before), 1Ti 6:5 (truth, 1 before), Phili 4:8 (honorable and just, 13 to 15 before) and Gen 18:28 (the righteous in the case put). The thought is a judgment whether something is right, true or lawful.

- **Observation 22532** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): War, return and the endurance of a held position stand with the thought in 2Ki 18:20 and Isa 36:5 (war, 5 after), 1Sa 18:17 (fight and battles), Mic 2:8 (war, 1 after), Jer 3:7 (return, 5 after, expected and not done), Heb 11:15 (return, 8 after, what thinking of the land would have allowed), 2Ch 13:8 (withstand, 1 after) and 1Cor 7:26 (remain, 9 after). The thought is of conflict, of return or of holding out.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): 1Sa 18:17 (thought), 2Ki 18:20 (think), 2Ch 13:8 (think), Isa 36:5 (think), Jer 3:7 (thought), Mic 2:8 (thought), 1Cor 7:26 (think), Heb 11:15 (thinking)
- **Observation 22533** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Small words stand in the units of the surfaces of this group and do not carry the sense of supposing: the connective G1161 in 1Cor 8:2, Rom 2:3 and 2Pe 1:13, G2532 in Rom 1:21, the particles and pronouns H3588A (Gen 20:11), H1931 (Pro 21:29), H0834A (Eze 20:32), H0853 (Neh 6:9), the preposition H0413 (Jer 40:4), the copula H1961 (Deu 15:9) and the returning H7725G (Mic 2:8). - **Observation 22534** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In the verses of the quoted thought the partner collections hold words that are not words of their collection's meaning: the surface 'surely' stands as a member of 'hear' in Exo 2:14, Jdg 3:24, 1Sa 16:6, 1Sa 20:26 and 2Ki 5:11; 'unworthy' as a member of 'reward' in Deu 15:9; 'presence' and 'present' as members of 'offer' in Rut 4:4 and 1Cor 7:26; and 'will' as a member of 'desire' in 2Sa 14:17 and 2Ki 7:12. These are recorded as open item #2536 and are not resolved here.
  - applies to 10 occurrence(s): Exo 2:14 (thought), Deu 15:9 (thought), Judg 3:24 (thought), Rut 4:4 (thought), 1Sa 16:6 (thought), 1Sa 20:26 (thought), 2Sa 14:17 (thought), 2Ki 5:11 (thought), 2Ki 7:12 (thinking), 1Cor 7:26 (think)
- **Observation 22535** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Thoughts come out of or arise in a person: evil thoughts come out of the heart in Mat 15:19 and Mar 7:21 (the heart 3 to 4 words before, the thoughts the first of the things named), thoughts will come into the mind in Eze 38:10, and thoughts came to the king on his bed in Dan 2:29. The act is the arising of a thought in the heart or mind, and in the first two it issues in the acts that the verses list after it.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Eze 38:10 (thoughts), Dan 2:29 (thoughts), Mat 15:19 (thoughts), Mar 7:21 (thoughts)
- **Observation 22536** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Another knows, perceives or discerns the thoughts: Job knows his friends' thoughts (Job 21:27), the Lord knows the thoughts of man (Psa 94:11), you discern my thoughts (Psa 139:2), Jesus knows or perceives theirs (Mat 9:4, Mat 12:25, Luk 5:22, Luk 6:8, Luk 11:17), the Lord knows what the wise think and finds it futile (1Cor 3:20), the Spirit knows a person's (1Cor 2:11) and the word of God discerns them (Heb 4:12). The verb of knowing stands within four words before the member in most of the verses where its position is stored.
  - applies to 11 occurrence(s): Job 21:27 (thoughts), Psa 94:11 (thoughts), Psa 139:2 (thoughts), Mat 9:4 (thoughts), Mat 12:25 (thoughts), Luk 5:22 (thoughts), Luk 6:8 (thoughts), Luk 11:17 (thoughts), 1Cor 2:11 (thoughts), 1Cor 3:20 (thoughts), Heb 4:12 (thoughts)
- **Observation 22537** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The thoughts alarm the one who has them in Dan 4:19, Dan 5:6, Dan 5:10 and Dan 7:28: the thoughts are the subject of the alarming, the verb stands 1 to 2 words after the member, and the change of color is named within five words. The act runs from the thoughts to the person.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Dan 4:19 (thoughts), Dan 5:6 (thoughts), Dan 5:10 (thoughts), Dan 7:28 (thoughts)
- **Observation 22538** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): An opinion is declared in Job 32:6, 32:10 and 32:17 (Elihu says he will declare his opinion, the declaring 2 words before) and laid bare in Pro 18:2 (the fool takes pleasure in expressing his heart). The opinion is a view held and then spoken, in the first three in an answer within a dialogue.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Job 32:6 (opinion), Job 32:10 (opinion), Job 32:17 (opinion), Pro 18:2 (opinion)
- **Observation 22539** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The Lord's own thoughts are stated as done and known by those who hear: his thoughts toward us are multiplied (Psa 40:5), very deep (Psa 92:5), precious (Psa 139:17), higher than ours (Isa 55:8, Isa 55:9) and not known by the nations (Mic 4:12, plan 4 words after). The Lord's thoughts are named beside his works in Psa 92:5 and Isa 66:18.
  - applies to 7 occurrence(s): Psa 40:5 (thoughts), Psa 92:5 (thoughts), Psa 139:17 (thoughts), Isa 55:8 (thoughts), Isa 55:9 (thoughts), Isa 66:18 (thoughts), Mic 4:12 (thoughts)
- **Observation 22540** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Evil and iniquitous thoughts are held or planned in Gen 6:5, Job 21:27, Psa 56:5, Pro 15:26, Isa 55:7, Isa 59:7, Jer 4:14 and Psa 10:4, with 'evil' or 'wicked' within five words of the member in each. The thoughts are of the wicked and the verses name the thought and the evil together.
  - applies to 8 occurrence(s): Gen 6:5 (thoughts), Job 21:27 (thoughts), Psa 10:4 (thoughts), Psa 56:5 (thoughts), Pro 15:26 (thoughts), Isa 55:7 (thoughts), Isa 59:7 (thoughts), Jer 4:14 (thoughts)
- **Observation 22541** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): A thought is asked to be searched and known in Psa 139:23 (search me and know my thoughts, the knowing 2 words before) and a person is told to forsake his thoughts in Isa 55:7 (the unrighteous man his thoughts, 5 words after the wicked). The first is asked of God and the second is said by the Lord of a person.
  - applies to 2 occurrence(s): Psa 139:23 (thoughts), Isa 55:7 (thoughts)
- **Observation 22542** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The thoughts are not known, not the same, not cared about: the nations do not know what the Lord thinks (Mic 4:12, the negator 2 words before the member), the Lord's thoughts are not our thoughts (Isa 55:8), and you do not care about anyone's opinion (Mat 22:16, Mar 12:14, the negator reaches 'care'). In each the thought or opinion is the object of a negated knowing, sameness or care.
  - applies to 4 occurrence(s): Isa 55:8 (thoughts), Mic 4:12 (thoughts), Mat 22:16 (opinion), Mar 12:14 (opinion)
- **Observation 22543** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Where the thoughts of persons are given up, set against God's or left uncared for, the act is a refusal as the verses state it: the unrighteous man is to forsake his thoughts (Isa 55:7) and the opinion of persons is not regarded (Mat 22:16, Mar 12:14). Where the psalmist asks to be known the act is a pursuit.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Isa 55:7 (thoughts), Mat 22:16 (opinion), Mar 12:14 (opinion)
- **Observation 22544** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Where the psalmist asks that his thoughts be known and tried (Psa 139:23) and speaks of the Lord's thoughts as precious (Psa 139:17) the act is a pursuit of what is asked or valued.
  - applies to 2 occurrence(s): Psa 139:17 (thoughts), Psa 139:23 (thoughts)
- **Observation 22545** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The thoughts are of iniquity in Isa 59:7, of evil in Gen 6:5, against the speaker in Psa 56:5, the words 'there is no God' in Psa 10:4, the wicked's in Pro 15:26 and the righteous's in Pro 12:5 (just), toward us in Psa 40:5 and of the Lord in Mic 4:12; the opinion in Mat 22:16 and Mar 12:14 is of persons and what they appear to be. The object or content of the thought is named in the verse or set beside the word within three words.
  - applies to 10 occurrence(s): Gen 6:5 (thoughts), Psa 10:4 (thoughts), Psa 40:5 (thoughts), Psa 56:5 (thoughts), Pro 12:5 (thoughts), Pro 15:26 (thoughts), Isa 59:7 (thoughts), Mic 4:12 (thoughts), Mat 22:16 (opinion), Mar 12:14 (opinion)
- **Observation 22546** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The evil thoughts come out of the heart together with the acts the verses list after them: sexual immorality 1 to 3 words after the member in Mat 15:19 and Mar 7:21, with murder, adultery, theft, false witness and slander in the same list. The thought is the first of the things that come out and the acts follow it in the same sentence.
  - applies to 2 occurrence(s): Mat 15:19 (thoughts), Mar 7:21 (thoughts)
- **Observation 22547** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): A heart or mind is named with the thoughts in all fifteen verses of the pairing: within three words in Gen 6:5, 1Ch 29:18, Dan 2:30, Luk 1:51, Eze 38:10, Mat 15:19, Luk 2:35 and Heb 4:12, and 4 to 9 away in Psa 139:23, Mar 7:21, Jer 4:14, Mat 9:4, Luk 5:22 and Dan 7:28; in Rom 2:15 the conscience stands 3 words before. The thoughts are placed in the heart, mind or conscience of the person.
  - applies to 15 occurrence(s): Gen 6:5 (thoughts), 1Ch 29:18 (thoughts), Psa 139:23 (thoughts), Jer 4:14 (thoughts), Eze 38:10 (thoughts), Dan 2:30 (thoughts), Dan 7:28 (thoughts), Mat 9:4 (thoughts), Mat 15:19 (thoughts), Mar 7:21 (thoughts), Luk 1:51 (thoughts), Luk 2:35 (thoughts); and 3 more (sheet `Observation nodes`)
- **Observation 22548** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Wealth, teaching and counsel stand with the thought in a few verses: the imagination is the wall of the rich man's wealth (Pro 18:11, wealth 6 before), the thoughts are not to be cursed in even against the rich (Ecc 10:20, rich 7 after), the counsels of the wicked stand beside the thoughts of the righteous (Pro 12:5, 3 after), and the opinion of persons is set against teaching the way of God (Mat 22:16, Mar 12:14). The thought is set beside the wealth, the counsel or the teaching of the one who holds it.
  - applies to 5 occurrence(s): Pro 12:5 (thoughts), Pro 18:11 (imagination), Ecc 10:20 (thoughts), Mat 22:16 (opinion), Mar 12:14 (opinion)
- **Observation 22549** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Qualifiers of the thoughts are 'evil' directly before the noun in Mat 15:19, Mar 7:21 and Jam 2:4 (1 word before), 'wicked' in Jer 4:14 (1 before), 'very deep' in Psa 92:5 and 'of the righteous' and 'of the wicked' in Pro 12:5 and Pro 15:26. The adjective gives the thought its moral character in the verses of evil and its depth in the verse of the Lord's thoughts.
  - applies to 7 occurrence(s): Psa 92:5 (thoughts), Pro 12:5 (thoughts), Pro 15:26 (thoughts), Jer 4:14 (thoughts), Mat 15:19 (thoughts), Mar 7:21 (thoughts), Jam 2:4 (thoughts)
- **Observation 22550** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Codes of the surfaces of this group have senses of their own that differ from 'thought': H8312 in Psa 139:23 (stored sense head 'anxiety'), H5587B in Job 20:2 (disquietings), H1902H in Lam 3:62 (the muttered musing of enemies), H1697I in Eze 38:10 (matters that come up), G0991 in Mat 22:16 and Mar 12:14 (a looking at persons, not an opinion held) and G3588 in 1Cor 2:11 (the article, morph empty, flagged in #2533). They are recorded as they stand in their verses.
  - applies to 7 occurrence(s): Job 20:2 (thoughts), Psa 139:23 (thoughts), Lam 3:62 (thoughts), Eze 38:10 (thoughts), Mat 22:16 (opinion), Mar 12:14 (opinion), 1Cor 2:11 (thoughts)
- **Observation 22551** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Conceiving is foreseen as an announcement in Jdg 13:3, 13:5 and 13:7, Isa 7:14, Mat 1:23 and Luk 1:31 (you shall conceive and bear a son), made by the angel of the Lord, by the Lord or by an angel to a woman, and as a condition in Num 5:28 (a clean woman shall conceive). The act stands in the future and is paired with bearing a son in each of the announcements.
  - applies to 7 occurrence(s): Num 5:28 (conceive), Judg 13:3 (conceive), Judg 13:5 (conceive), Judg 13:7 (conceive), Isa 7:14 (conceive), Mat 1:23 (conceive), Luk 1:31 (conceive)
- **Observation 22552** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Conceiving is a done act in Psa 51:5 (the speaker's mother conceived him) and in Heb 11:11 (Sarah received power to conceive); in the Greek verse the conceiving is carried by a phrase of two nouns, 'laying down' and 'seed', and in Mat 1:23 by the verb 'to have' with 'in'. The act is bodily and is stated of a woman in each verse.
  - applies to 2 occurrence(s): Psa 51:5 (conceive), Heb 11:11 (conceive)
- **Observation 22553** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The figurative conceiving of what is then borne is a done act in Job 15:35 (trouble conceived and evil borne), Isa 33:11 (chaff conceived and stubble borne) and Isa 59:4 (mischief conceived and iniquity borne). The same code H2029 whose Poel sense is to contrive or devise is used of bodily conceiving in Jdg 13:3; in these three verses what is conceived is evil and what is borne is evil or stubble.
  - applies to 3 occurrence(s): Job 15:35 (conceive), Isa 33:11 (conceive), Isa 59:4 (conceive)
- **Observation 22554** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In Num 11:12 the conceiving is put as a rhetorical question: Moses asks whether he conceived all this people and gave them birth. The question presupposes that he did not, so the conceiving is stated as not done by him and the bodily word is used of his relation to the people.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Num 11:12 (conceive)
- **Observation 22555** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Devising harm is a done act in Psa 41:7: those who hate the speaker whisper together and imagine the worst for him. The verb is the code H2803I whose lexicon sense is to plan or devise, and the unit also holds the preposition H5921A, 'upon', beside it.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Psa 41:7 (imagine)
- **Observation 22556** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The devising of Psa 41:7 is a pursuit of harm: the object is the worst for the speaker, and the verb is set among whispering and hating.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Psa 41:7 (imagine)
- **Observation 22557** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): In Num 11:12 the act is a refusal as the question puts it: Moses denies having conceived the people and so denies the charge laid on him.
  - applies to 1 occurrence(s): Num 11:12 (conceive)
- **Observation 22558** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): What is conceived is a son in Jdg 13:5, 13:7, Isa 7:14, Mat 1:23 and Luk 1:31, children in Num 5:28, the speaker in Psa 51:5, a people in Num 11:12, trouble, chaff and mischief in Job 15:35, Isa 33:11 and Isa 59:4, and the worst against the speaker in Psa 41:7. The conceiving of a child is the commoner object across the verses.
  - applies to 12 occurrence(s): Num 5:28 (conceive), Num 11:12 (conceive), Judg 13:5 (conceive), Judg 13:7 (conceive), Job 15:35 (conceive), Psa 41:7 (imagine), Psa 51:5 (conceive), Isa 7:14 (conceive), Isa 33:11 (conceive), Isa 59:4 (conceive), Mat 1:23 (conceive), Luk 1:31 (conceive)
- **Observation 22559** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Bearing follows the conceiving in six verses: 1 word after the member in Jdg 13:3, 13:5, 13:7 and Isa 7:14, 2 after in Mat 1:23 and 4 after in Luk 1:31; and calling the child's name follows in Isa 7:14 (3 after), Mat 1:23 (5) and Luk 1:31 (7). The conceiving is the first act of a pair or a series, bearing and naming.
  - applies to 6 occurrence(s): Judg 13:3 (conceive), Judg 13:5 (conceive), Judg 13:7 (conceive), Isa 7:14 (conceive), Mat 1:23 (conceive), Luk 1:31 (conceive)
- **Observation 22560** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The conceiving is stated of women whose condition the verse names: barren and not having borne (Jdg 13:3), clean and not defiled (Num 5:28), and in Heb 11:11 Sarah past the age; the negators of these verses reach the words defiled and borne, and in Jdg 13:5 and 13:7 they reach what the woman must not do (a razor, wine, unclean food). The conceiving is announced under a condition of the woman.
  - applies to 5 occurrence(s): Num 5:28 (conceive), Judg 13:3 (conceive), Judg 13:5 (conceive), Judg 13:7 (conceive), Heb 11:11 (conceive)
- **Observation 22561** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): The divine and the human are named in eight verses: God (4), the Lord and Jesus, with Immanuel (3), Sarah (2), the woman and the child. The messenger or the Lord announces the conceiving to the woman, the woman conceives and bears a son, and the child is called by the name the Lord gives; the direction of the announcement does not change across the verses.
  - applies to 5 occurrence(s): Judg 13:5 (conceive), Isa 7:14 (conceive), Mat 1:23 (conceive), Luk 1:31 (conceive), Heb 11:11 (conceive)
- **Observation 22562** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Small words stand in the units of the surfaces of this group and do not carry the sense of conceiving or devising: the preposition H5921A in Psa 41:7, the Greek 'in' (G1722) in Mat 1:23 and the verb 'to have' (G2192) read with it, which together form the idiom rendered conceive. They are read in their verses as standalone beside the member that gives the sense; the grouping placed the whole surface 'conceive' under devising although most of its occurrences are bodily conceiving, which was left as uncertain.
  - applies to 2 occurrence(s): Psa 41:7 (imagine), Mat 1:23 (conceive)
- **Observation 22563** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Behaviour discovered: holding a supposition about another person or a state of affairs, and answering or acting on it. The elements show a thought quoted to oneself or a supposition held (22506, 22507), its content stated (death, escape, peace, security, another's state, the Lord's love: 22514, 22515, 22517), the act or the spoken answer that follows it in the same verse (22513, 22519, 22520), and the circumstances that stand beside it: fear, anger, hiding, distress, knowing, trust, asking, searching, sin, truth and war (22521 to 22523, 22525 to 22529, 22531, 22532). They belong together because each is a part of one working: a supposition formed and then lived out. This gathers the elements ... (cut; the whole text is in the workbook)

- **Observation 22564** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Behaviour discovered: holding a view of oneself that the verse then tests. The elements show a person thinking he is wise, something, religious, a prophet, standing or knowing (22508), the object of the thinking being the thinker (22516), and the believing, honor, reward and commanding that stand beside it (22530). They belong together because the supposition is about the holder and the verse sets it beside deceiving oneself, folly or knowledge. - **Observation 22567** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Behaviour discovered: meditating on God, his word and his works. - **Observation 22568** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Behaviour discovered: the Lord considering persons and knowing their thoughts. They belong together because the considering is followed in the verse by what is concluded or what follows from it. This gathers the elements named by the traced observations and states no claim about what the behaviour is.

- **Observation 22570** (layer-3, tag statement, marker not yet marked, status draft): Behaviour discovered: reckoning or counting a person or thing as something, and the standing given.
